The step is 3.5 px in e (the game's speed), so targets fall on half pixels: round each frame's position so a planted
foot's moves alternate (3 and 4 px in e) and its total stays within 0.5 px of the target. targets.json "views" gives the

still, pixel for pixel, dipping 1 px on f0 and f4 (the frames where a pair lands). The tail is the still's tail moved
whole: it dips 1 px one frame later than the body (f1 and f5), never redrawn, never curled, and never drawn over a leg.
The legs are the still's own legs re-posed. Each leg is hip, upper leg, round steel knee disc, lower leg and steel foot.
The foot and lower leg move together to the target, the knee disc moves about half as far, and the upper leg between hip
and knee is redrawn as a leaning band in the still's own colours and shading bands, stepped 1 px per row or two, with its
lit edge and its dark seam kept. On a lifted foot the lower leg rises with it. Every part is the still's own pixels
TRANSLATED, never rotated or resampled (rotation left dark specks and slivers on the rhino). Every joint and every join
with the body (shoulder, hip, knee) is redrawn by hand: no seams, no straight cut edges, no gaps, no part ending abruptly.

# Walk playbook (walk lane)

How to make a machine's walk at 35 degrees, check it, and price it. Started 2026-11-05 by the walk lane ("Build mech
walk animations", Preston: "Get a walking animations session going that is skilled up from everything we learned").
Update this file when a lesson changes. The run log is `LOG.md` in this folder.

**Sources.** Read this session: `ANIMATION_PLAYBOOK.md` (all), `cam35_fleet/HANDOFF.md`, the rhino 35-degree walk
(`rhino_l/take-a/anim/walk35/`: REPORT.txt, clean/REPORT.txt, brief_run1.txt), the wolf walk4 run
(`wolf_m/take-b/anim/codex-walk4/REPORT.txt`, brief.txt, `common.txt`), the gorilla's set A walk report, the kits
(`docs/plan/moba-kits.md`), and the game feel lane's answers on speed (2026-11-05). General knowledge, not read
this session: the four key poses (Richard Williams, *The Animator's Survival Kit*), animal gait sequences
(Muybridge's plates, Hildebrand's gait studies), and the walk-to-run Froude rule (R. McNeill Alexander). Those
parts are marked as craft proposals. Nothing here is canon until Preston says so.

## 1. Rules that bind every walk
- **Build from the newest views.** Before any frames, look for `<take>/views_cam35_touchup/` and its APPROVED.txt.
  The rhino walk was drawn from `views_cam35` after Preston kept the touch-up, and needed `walk35_touchup.py` to
  recolour 40 frames afterwards.
- **The walk and the stills share one angle.** A machine's walk is drawn at 35 degrees only once its 35-degree stills
  are approved. Mixed angles pop at every start and stop.
- **Above the legs, every frame is the still**, moved only by the bob. Then idle to walk cannot pop. The rhino's
  35-degree walk matched its still on 0% differing pixels above the legs.
- **Legs are the still's own pixels, translated, never rotated or resampled.** Rotation left specks that cost a
  78,180-token cleanup on the rhino. Where a leg leans, step its parts 1 px a row.
- **Joints are redrawn, not pasted.** Pasted parts that did not join caused the cutoffs Preston rejected ("Too many
  cutoff etc."). Every joint and every join with the body is drawn in the still's colours and bands.
- **Feet stay planted** (section 7), and the drawn stride is checked against the game's speed (section 8).
- **No curled tails.** Tails lift and swing but stay straight, with the still's thickness and plate count.
- **Palette, canvas, offsets and feet row are the machine's own.** Keep its `palette.json`; place stills on the 192
  canvas at (+16, 160 - feet row).
- **Judge at 4x with the game's sel-out, the approved still in column 1.** He rejects "barely acceptable".
- **Never write into another lane's folders or the approved views.** This lane writes to `walk/<machine>/` only.
- **The session worktree has no `docs/art/moba`.** Write files in the worktree and copy them over with Bash (the
  fleet lane's method). C: was full on 2026-11-05, so the scratchpad was not usable.

## 2. Key poses (craft, proposal)
A walk is a controlled fall caught by each landing foot. Per foot, four poses:

| Pose | What happens | What the body does |
|---|---|---|
| Contact | The swinging foot lands ahead; the other foot of that pair is at the back of its stance | Hold the still's height |
| Down | Weight drops onto the landing foot | The dip: the body sinks 1 px (L can take 2); the shoulders or hips over that foot lead |
| Passing | The swinging foot passes the planted one, at its highest lift | The body returns to still height |
| Up | The planted foot pushes off | Body at still height (rising over it would lift the head off the still; not used yet) |

- On our machines the body only translates by whole pixels; armour does not squash. The rhino's approved bob is a
  1 px dip on f0 and f4 (the frames a fore foot lands).
- Head and tail lag the body by one frame (section 5). The rhino's pose-guided front walk leaned the head toward the
  planted foot, and Preston liked that walk.

## 3. Gaits by body (proposals unless marked approved)
| Machine | Gait | Phase per 8 frames | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| rhino (L) | four-beat lateral walk | LF swings f1-f3, LH f3-f5, RF f5-f7, RH f7-f1; one foot up on even frames, two on odd; lifts 2, 3, 2 | approved, installed at 35 |
| gorilla (L) | knuckle walk, diagonal pairs | right fist + left foot planted f0-f3, the other pair f4-f7; the fists swing as whole forearms | the set A walk (old angle) used diagonal pairs; the kit says "knuckle walking". Real gorillas use a four-beat walk: both are on the guide for Preston |
| wolf (M) | trot, diagonal pairs | pair A (near fore + far hind) planted f0-f3, pair B f4-f7; lifts 2, 4, 4, 2 | approved at the old angle |
| fennec (S) | trot | 10 fps, STRIDE 0.56 m/s in `fennec_kit.gd` | approved at the old angle; the walk is exempt from f0 = still (seated still) |
| hippo (L), pangolin (M) | four-beat walk, like the rhino | as rhino | proposal |
| chameleon (M) | slow diagonal-pair walk with a side-to-side rock | as wolf, longer holds | proposal |
| kangaroo (M) | hop: both hind feet together, tail and gloves trail | take-off f0-f1, air f2-f5, land f6-f7 | proposal; kit: "big hops" |
| squirrel (S) | hop or bound | as kangaroo, shorter air | proposal; kit: "hops and sudden changes of direction" |
| shoebill (M) | two legs: L planted f0-f3, R f4-f7, a body sway toward the planted leg | 8 frames | proposal |
| hummingbird (S) | no walk: hover drift (wing cycle plus a lean into the move) | wing cycle | kit: "hovering"; check with game feel |
| falcon (M) | check the kit and game feel (walk on talons, or a low glide) | | open |

## 4. Weight and timing by size (proposal; the rhino, wolf and fennec numbers are real)
| Size | Frames | ms a frame | Cycle | Dip | Lifts |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| L | 8 | 125 (8 fps), rhino | 1.0 s | 1 px (2 px on a heavy landing, such as the gorilla dropping onto its fists) | 2-3 px |
| M | 8 | 100 (10 fps), wolf | 0.8 s | 1 px | 2-4 px |
| S | 8 | 100 (10 fps), fennec | 0.8 s | 0-1 px | 3-4 px |

Heavier means longer holds on the planted feet, a lower down pose and smaller lifts. Lighter means more air and a
quicker cycle. A cycle length is a placeholder until game feel and Preston set the speed (section 8).

## 5. Overlap and secondary motion
- **Tails:** straight. They trail the body bob by one frame and swing 1-2 px sideways, opposite the hips.
- **Ears, fins, antennae and cables:** trail by one frame, 1 px. Rigid armour plates move with their body part and
  never flex.
- **Gems, lenses and eyes:** carried with their part by translation, never redrawn smaller. Check them on every frame
  (the gorilla's hex gems broke in its hex_shield ne frames).
- **Arms that are legs** (gorilla fists, kangaroo gloves): the whole forearm block moves, and the shoulder join is
  redrawn.

## 6. Loops, frames and views
- **Seamless loop:** f7 to f0 is a normal step. Each foot does exactly one swing per cycle, the planted step is the
  same across the wrap, and the dip pattern repeats. Check it with an onion skin of f7 under f0.
- **Frames:** 8 a cycle, equal timing. Walks are retimed only with Preston's word (the wolf front and back were
  retimed into pairs on f0 and f4).
- **Views:** draw s, se, e, ne and n. The engine mirrors sw, w and nw, and a turn keeps the frame index.
- **Mirror phase (in the game since 2026-11-05, Preston: "Yes, fix it"):** a mirror swaps left and right, so in sw,
  w and nw the foot that is up is the other one. `show_facing()` in `scripts/moba3d/directional_sprite_3d.gd` now
  moves a walking machine's frame to (i + 4) mod 8 (minions (i + 2) mod 4) when it flips into or out of a mirrored
  view, the e-to-w snap included; idle and actions are untouched. Proof: `tools/debug/walk_turn_check.gd`, gorilla
  foot swaps 3 to 0. So every alternating walk (four-beat, diagonal, biped) must keep its two halves symmetric: the
  foot that swings on f(i) swings on its partner's side on f(i + 4), and the dip lands on f0 and f4. A hop has no
  left-right half, so check how a hop looks across that half-loop jump before drawing one. `walk_tools.py mirror`
  checks a gait.

## 7. Foot lock at 35 degrees
- A ground step of d texels along the body shows on screen as d x f x u. At pitch p and view yaw a (e 0, se 45,
  s 90), the screen vector is (cos a, sin a x sin p). At 35: e u (1, 0), f 1.000; se u (0.867, 0.497), f 0.815;
  s u (0, 1), f 0.574; ne and n the same with y negated.
- **Planted step a frame on the canvas = -P x f x u**, where P is the side-view step in px a frame. At P = 3:
  e (-3, 0); se (-2.12, -1.22); ne (-2.12, +1.22); s (0, -1.72); n (0, +1.72).
- **Lock rule:** the k-th planted frame's sole centre minus k planted steps stays within 2 px of the first. The rhino
  passed with e 0.00, se and ne 0.88, s and n 0.88 px.
- **Depth travel shows now.** At 35, a fore-and-back step in s and n shows at 0.574 of its length (0.16 at the old
  angle), so front and back walks need real foot travel, not reordered frames.
- **Toes toward the camera** may draw 2-3 px under the feet row (rhino s and n 163, se and ne 162). The floor hides
  none of it, and walk and idle both end on row 161 with sel-out.
- **The game camera is 30 degrees and the art is drawn at 35** (game feel, 2026-11-05). An s or n planted step drawn
  for 35 plays about 13% long in game: 0.22 px a frame at P = 3. That is under the 2 px lock gate; logged for game
  feel.

## 8. Stride against speed
- Drawn ground speed = P x fps x texel (m). Texel: L 0.020, M 0.018, S 0.016 m.
- Rhino: 3 x 8 x 0.020 = 0.48 m/s, kept by Preston ("Keep 0.48 m/s").
- The game: `move_speed = 3.5` m/s for every machine, a placeholder (`machine_test_player.gd:7`). `stride_speed`
  is one export for all machines (0.48), and `full_speed_walk_rate` is still 1.0, so the feet slide today.
- To plant at 3.5 m/s, a walk drawn at 0.48 m/s plays 7.3 times fast, or the side step must be 21.9 px a frame at
  L and 8 fps (24.3 at M, 27.3 at S). That is a run, not a walk: the gorilla's whole side view is 79 px long.
- **Natural walk speed (Froude, proposal):** v = sqrt(Fr x 9.81 x leg height in m). A comfortable walk is about
  Fr 0.25; animals switch to a run near Fr 0.5. `walk_tools.py stride` prints this per machine from its leg height.
- **Preston picks**, per game feel: lower move_speed, play the walk fast, draw longer strides, or add a run cycle.
  This lane never picks a speed. `stride_speed` needs a per-machine value once a second machine walks.

## 9. The checks on every walk (`walk_tools.py footlock`, plus the eye)
| Check | Pass |
|---|---|
| Foot lock per visible foot, from the frame pixels | spread 2 px or under in x and y |
| Swing feet | lift matches the gait sheet within 1 px; the swing goes forward along u |
| Above the legs | 0% differing pixels against the still after the dip |
| Lowest row per frame | the feet row, or 2-3 rows under it for toes toward the camera, listed |
| Palette, alpha, holes, strays, canvas edge | the still's colours only, binary alpha, one connected machine, no edge contact |
| Seams (`walk_tools.py seams`) | no slit where a limb slid back from the pinned body (solid in the idle, now an enclosed hole). Codex left them on the gorilla in e, se and ne; one `--fix` pass filled them with the body bands. Look first: open space beside a far limb at the silhouette edge is real and stays open, so never run a second pass |
| Loop | f7 to f0 is a normal step (planted step within 1 px of the others) |
| Mirror phase | the same physical foot up across s/sw and n/nw at each frame, or the offset noted |
| Stride | drawn m/s, and the playback rate needed at the current move_speed, reported |
| Eye | board at 4x with sel-out, still in column 1; a dot-grid review video (`make_walk35_review.py`) |
| Game | packed by the animation lane, then game feel's turn and speed test |

The foot-lock tool finds feet by the bottom of each column, so a foot hidden behind the body cannot be measured from a
composite. Codex's own verify measured those from its limb layers; ask for them in the brief.

## 11. A walk, step by step
1. Confirm the 35-degree stills are approved, and find the newest folder (touch-up first).
2. `walk_tools.py stride` with the machine's leg height and size; agree P and fps with game feel.
3. `walk_tools.py gait` writes `targets.json`, the guides and the plan video for Preston. Free.
4. Price the Codex run (section 12) and wait for Preston's yes in his own words.
5. Codex run 1 (e, se, ne), then run 2 (s, n), each under a token cap, with the rhino's brief_run1 rules: translate,
   never rotate; joints by hand; body pinned.
6. `walk_tools.py footlock` and `seams` on every view, then the board at 4x with sel-out and a 5x zoom of each
   moving joint. The gorilla's cut-off ne limb passed every number and showed only in the zoom. Add to every brief:
   "where a limb slides back, draw the body behind it; no slit may show the background inside the body".
7. Send the frames to the animation lane to pack, to game feel for the turn and speed test, and to the touch-up
   lane for cleanup. One line to the hub.

## 12. Pricing a walk
| Run | Rhino tokens | Expected with the lessons built in |
|---|---|---|
| Run 1: e, se, ne | 102,921 | 95k-115k |
| Leg cleanup | 78,180 (caused by rotation) | 0 if run 1 translates; 60k-80k if not |
| Run 2: s, n | 93,859 | 85k-105k |
| Repair pass for cutoffs (fennec) | 45k-190k for two sets | only if the board shows cutoffs |
You are making pixel-art walk frames for a game. Write only inside D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run1.
Read anything under D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba. Do not touch any other folder, assets, art or tools, and do not
commit. Run tag: ape-walk35-run1.

The machine: the green L gorilla (mech). Its approved stills at 35 degrees are in
D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\ape_l\take-a\views_cam35_touchup (s, se, e, ne, n; 160x160, feet row 130, 15 colours,
raw with no outline). The same stills placed on the game's 192x192 canvas (at +16, +30, so the lowest foot is on row
160) are in run1\stills; work on those. Preston, the game's owner, approved these stills; they are the idle in the
game, so the walk must match them exactly above the limbs or the switch from idle to walk pops.
Read first: D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\WALK_PLAYBOOK.md (sections 1, 5, 7 and 9),
D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\ANIMATION_PLAYBOOK.md (sections 1, 4, 5 and 9), and the rhino's 35-degree walk, the
method that worked last: D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\rhino_l\take-a\anim\walk35 (brief_run1.txt, REPORT.txt,
build.py, verify.py, and clean\REPORT.txt with cleanup.py for what went wrong there). The gorilla's earlier walk at the
old angle is in D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\ape_l\take-a\anim\codex-set-a (how its fists and forearms moved).

Job, run 1: an 8-frame walk, 125 ms a frame (8 fps), looping, in these views: e, se, ne. The gait Preston chose is a
knuckle walk in diagonal pairs: the right fist and left hind foot swing f5-f7; the left fist and right hind foot swing
f1-f3; both pairs are planted on f0 and f4. Each foot swings for 3 frames with lifts of 2, 3 and 2 px. Each foot's path
(offset along the body in side-view px, + = forward, and lift) is in run1\targets.json "paths"; per view the path turns
into screen px as offset * f * u, with the lift straight up:
  e: u = (1, 0), f = 1.0; a planted foot moves (-3.00, +0.00) px a frame on the canvas (x, y; y down)
  se: u = (0.8674, 0.4975), f = 0.8152; a planted foot moves (-2.12, -1.22) px a frame on the canvas
  ne: u = (0.8674, -0.4975), f = 0.8152; a planted foot moves (-2.12, +1.22) px a frame on the canvas
So a planted foot keeps one spot on the ground while the body moves forward. targets.json "views" gives the sole centre
of every foot visible in each still for every frame. run1\guide_<view>.png shows them: the still faded, then a bar per
foot per frame, filled when planted and outlined when lifted, with a tick for the lift. F = fist (the shielded forearms),
H = hind foot. The foot names are my reading of the stills; check them and say if one is wrong (the least certain is
LH in ne). A foot hidden in the still: draw it only where the new pose shows it (in se both hind feet are hidden).

Method: no image tool, native pixels, the way the rhino walk did it. The body, head, visor, back plate and shoulders are
the approved still, pixel for pixel, dipping 1 px on f0 and f4 (the frames where a pair lands). The limbs are the still's
own limbs re-posed: each forearm moves as one block with its fist and hex shield, and each hind leg as its thigh, shin
and foot. Every part is the still's own pixels TRANSLATED, never rotated or resampled (the rhino's first run rotated its
shanks, and the rotation left dark specks and slivers that a 78k-token cleanup had to remove). The hex shields and their
green gems are carried whole by translation, never redrawn smaller or reshaped (the gorilla's gems broke once, in its
hex_shield ne frames). Where a limb leans, step its parts 1 px per row or redraw the joint by hand in the still's
colours and bands. Every joint and every join with the body (shoulder, elbow, hip, knee) is redrawn by hand: no seams,
no straight cut edges, no gaps, no part ending abruptly. Pasted parts that did not join are what Preston rejected
before ("Too many cutoff etc."). Keep each limb's length within 1 px of the still's.

Pass marks (report each):
- Foot lock: for each foot, its sole centre on its k-th planted frame (k = 0 to 4 in order, wrapping past f7) minus k
  times the planted step above stays within 2 px in x and y. Measure from the frame pixels, not from your targets. Write
  a footprint picture per view. Also run this and report its result:
  python D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\tools\walk_tools.py footlock
    --targets D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run1\targets.json
    --frames D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run1\walk\frames --views e se ne
    --still-dir D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run1\stills
    --out D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run1\footlock.json
  For a foot hidden behind the body in a frame, measure it from your limb layer and say so.
- Each visible foot meets its target within 1 px; lifts exact.
- Above the limbs every frame equals the still (after the 1 px dip): report the % of differing pixels per frame.
- 192x192 transparent frames, the stills' 15 colours only, binary alpha, no holes, no stray pixels, nothing touching the
  canvas edge. A planted foot nearer the camera than in the still may sit up to 3 px below row 160 (perspective); report
  each frame's lowest row.
- Judge boards at 4x with the game's 1 px dark sel-out added (the packer adds it; use the selout function in
  D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\touchup\touchup_machine.py), with the still beside them.

Output in run1: walk\frames\<view>_f0.png to <view>_f7.png for e, se and ne (raw, no outline, like the stills), a preview
gif per view (125 ms, loop), footprint pictures, board.png (per view: the still, then your 8 frames, with sel-out, at
4x), checks.txt, footlock.json, and REPORT.txt (under 30 lines): tokens if known, the foot names you used, the
foot-lock spreads, target errors, upper-body match, lowest rows, and by eye: does each view read as this gorilla
knuckle-walking forward in diagonal pairs, with limbs like the still, whole and clean?
Gorilla walk at 35, run 1 (e, se, ne), knuckle walk in diagonal pairs. Written by the walk lane (Claude), 2026-11-05:
Codex was stopped by the token cap while finalizing, before it wrote its own report.

Tokens: 143,043 (cap 140,000; the watcher checks every 20 s). Estimate was 95k-115k.
Output: walk/frames/<view>_f0-7.png (24 frames), board.png, <view>_board.png, previews, footprints, checks.txt,
verification.json, footlock.json, build.py, verify.py, rig.json. Not written: delivery.json and Codex's REPORT.txt.

Foot lock (walk_tools.py footlock, measured by the walk lane from the frame pixels): every visible foot locked.
  e RH 0.00/0.00, RF 0.00/0.00 px; se RF, LF 0.48/0.87; ne LH, RH, RF 0.48/0.87. Gate 2 px.
  Codex's own stamp measure puts e RF within 0.5 px of target; walk_tools reads 3.0 px on one lifted frame (the
  knuckle slope; a tool limit, not a frame fault). Codex also tracked hidden feet from its limb layers (e LH).
Structure (walk lane): one piece per frame, except se f0 with 1 loose px at (135, 111), #8a8a93. Binary alpha, 15
  colours, no canvas edge. Lowest rows 157-162 (se and ne f0 reach 162, allowed for feet toward the camera).
Upper body: Codex reports 0 px differing outside its traced limbs on every frame. The walk lane's cruder check
  (top 45% of the still, limbs included) reads e 0.5-1.4%, se 3.7-11.2%, ne 3.0-8.9%; most of that is the moving
  forearms and shields, which reach that high on this machine.

By eye (2x board with sel-out, then 5x zoom):
  e and se read as the gorilla knuckle-walking forward in diagonal pairs. Shields and gems are whole in every frame.
  ne: the far-left limb (labelled LH) breaks up. In most frames its orange parts are cut off or detached, the grey
  knee disc is broken in f0-f3, and loose dark specks sit under that foot in f4-f7. Not acceptable as is. Zoom:
  D:\dev\_scratch\walk\ape_run1_ne_lowerleft.png. The ne LH label was the least certain one in the brief.

Next (Preston's call): fold an ne far-limb repair and the se f0 loose pixel into run 2 (s, n), so Codex reads the
playbooks once; or a separate ne repair pass (estimate 30k-60k). Not yet packed or tested in game.

This is run 2 of the green L gorilla's walk at 35 degrees. Run 1 (D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run1)
made the e, se and ne views. Read run1\brief.txt (the machine, the gait, the rules and the pass marks all still apply),
run1\REPORT.txt (the walk lane's review), and run1\build.py, verify.py, rig.json and checks.txt: that is your own
method from run 1, so reuse it rather than starting again. Read D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\WALK_PLAYBOOK.md
section 7 for the front and back views. The stills on the 192 canvas are in run2\stills; the targets (the same plan as
run 1, with s and n included) are in run2\targets.json, and the guides are run2\guide_s.png, guide_n.png and guide_ne.png.

Work in this order, and keep run2\REPORT.txt up to date after each part, so a report exists even if the run is stopped:

Part 1, repair ne. Preston saw run 1's ne frames: the far-left limb breaks up. In most frames its orange parts are cut
off or detached, the grey knee disc is broken in f0-f3, and loose dark specks sit under that foot in f4-f7. A 5x zoom of
the region (x 38-96, y 112-166 on the 192 canvas; still, then f0-f7) is run2\ne_defects_run1.png. Check first which limb
this is (the brief called it LH, the least certain label; the shield edge just above it suggests the far forearm may be
involved) and fix the label if it is wrong. Then redraw that limb in all 8 ne frames from the still's own parts:
translated, never rotated, every joint and the join with the body drawn by hand, nothing cut off, no floating pieces, no
specks. Keep everything else in run 1's ne frames exactly as it is, and keep every foot's lock. Write the repaired frames
to run2\walk\frames\ne_f0.png to ne_f7.png. (The loose pixel in se f0 is already fixed: run2\walk\frames\se_f0.png.)

Part 2, s. Part 3, n. Each is an 8-frame loop, 125 ms a frame, with the same diagonal-pair gait as run 1: the right fist
and left hind foot swing f5-f7, the left fist and right hind foot swing f1-f3, both pairs are planted on f0 and f4, and
the body dips 1 px on f0 and f4. Per view, the path turns into screen px as offset * f * u, with the lift straight up:
  s: u = (0, 1), f = 0.5736; a planted foot moves (0.00, -1.72) px a frame on the canvas (x, y; y down)
  n: u = (0, -1), f = 0.5736; a planted foot moves (0.00, +1.72) px a frame on the canvas
At 35 degrees a step toward or away from the camera shows at 0.57 of its length, so these views need real foot travel
up and down the screen, not reordered side frames. In s the fists are the near feet (row 160) and the hind feet sit
between them (row 143); in n the hind feet are near (row 160) and the fists far (row 144). A foot nearer the camera than
in the still may sit up to 3 px below row 160. The forearm blocks with their hex shields and gems move whole, by
translation; in s and n the shields face the sides, so check that each one stays whole in every frame.

Pass marks, for ne, s and n: as in run1\brief.txt. Run this and report its result:
  python D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\tools\walk_tools.py footlock
    --targets D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run2\targets.json
    --frames D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run2\walk\frames --views ne s n
    --still-dir D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run2\stills
    --out D:\dev\new-mech-rpg\docs\art\moba\walk\ape_l\run2\footlock.json
For ne, also make a 5x zoom of the repaired region laid out like ne_defects_run1.png: ne_repaired.png.

def fill_holes(op):
 seen=op.copy();queue=deque()
 for x in range(192):
  for y in (0,191):
   if not seen[y,x]:seen[y,x]=True;queue.append((y,x))
 for y in range(192):
  for x in (0,191):
   if not seen[y,x]:seen[y,x]=True;queue.append((y,x))
 while queue:
  y,x=queue.popleft()
  for yy,xx in ((y-1,x),(y+1,x),(y,x-1),(y,x+1)):
   if 0<=yy<192 and 0<=xx<192 and not seen[yy,xx]:seen[yy,xx]=True;queue.append((yy,xx))
 return ~seen|op

def mask(poly):
 im=Image.new('1',(192,192));ImageDraw.Draw(im).polygon(poly,fill=1);return np.array(im)
def shift(a,dx=0,dy=0):
 b=np.zeros_like(a);ys,xs=np.where(a[:,:,3]>0);xx=xs+int(dx);yy=ys+int(dy);ok=(xx>=0)&(xx<192)&(yy>=0)&(yy<192);b[yy[ok],xx[ok]]=a[ys[ok],xs[ok]];return b
def comp(a,b):
 m=b[:,:,3]>0;a[m]=b[m]
def piece(a,m):
 b=a.copy();b[~m]=0;return b
def support(a):
 yy,xx=np.where(a[:,:,3]>0);row=int(yy.max());xs=np.where(a[row-1:row+1,:,3].any(axis=0))[0];return np.array([(xs.min()+xs.max())/2,row],float)
def poly(a,points,c):
 im=Image.fromarray(a);ImageDraw.Draw(im).polygon([tuple(map(int,p)) for p in points],fill=P[c]);a[:]=im

def socket(q,dx,dy,dip):
 # Short green articulated link behind the intact native hinge. Horizontal
 # colour bands follow a one-pixel staircase, with round stepped end caps.
 a=np.zeros((192,192,4),np.uint8)
 x0,y0=q['root'];x1,y1=q['joint'];y0+=dip;x1+=dx;y1+=dy
 w=q['w'];height=max(1,y1-y0)
 for y in range(y0,y1+3):
  t=np.clip((y-y0)/height,0,1);cx=int(np.floor(x0+(x1-x0)*t+.5))
  inset=2 if y in (y0,y1+2) else 1 if y in (y0+1,y1+1) else 0
  l,r=cx-w+inset,cx+w-inset
  a[y,l:r+1]=P['22301d'];a[y,l+1:r]=P['47693a'];a[y,l+1:cx+1]=P['6c9b4a']
  if y<y0+3:a[y,l+2:min(cx,l+4)]=P['9ccb62']
 return a

def load_selout():
 # touchup_machine imports this exact function; no module top-level code is run.
 sp=B/'rhino_l/take-a/anim/selout_rhino.py'
 tree=ast.parse(sp.read_text());ns={'np':np,'OUTLINE':np.array([20,26,22],np.uint8)}
 exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='selout'],type_ignores=[]),str(sp),'exec'),ns)
 return ns['selout']

def sources(view,ref):
 src={};used=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
 # Forearm overlaps own hind limb. All source texels get a single owner.
 for name in ['RF','LF','RH','LH']:
  if name not in D[view]:continue
  traced=mask(D[view][name]['poly'])
  # Include every native contour texel at plate corners, avoiding pinned slivers.
  if (view,name)==('e','RH'):traced |= mask([(61,119),(70,118),(78,120),(83,126),(84,134),(86,140),(86,152),(56,152),(55,137),(58,132),(58,124)])
  if (view,name)==('e','LF'):traced |= mask([(118,112),(124,112),(130,114),(134,117),(136,124),(136,133),(123,134),(121,126),(114,124),(113,116)])
  if (view,name)==('se','LF'):
   traced=mask([(124,102),(127,104),(131,108),(136,111),(140,116),(142,130),(143,143),(132,150),(120,150),(112,142),(110,124),(113,120),(119,119),(121,116),(123,112),(123,107)])
   D[view][name]['root']=(124,101);D[view][name]['joint']=(126,113);D[view][name]['w']=4
  if (view,name)==('se','LH'):traced |= (X>=97)&(X<=101)&(Y>=136)&(Y<=139)
  if (view,name)==('ne','RH'):traced |= mask([(81,133),(87,129),(94,131),(99,137),(101,145),(104,149),(104,162),(77,162),(75,154),(76,146),(76,140)])
  if (view,name)==('ne','RF'):traced |= ((X>=104)&(Y>=116))|((X>=122)&(Y>=113))
  m=traced&~used&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
  src[name]=piece(ref,m);used|=m
 body=piece(ref,~used)
 if view=='e':
  # Left hind is fully behind the right thigh/rump. Same native limb, depth lane.
  src['LH']=shift(src['RH'],0,-8)
  D[view]['LH']=dict(D[view]['RH']);D[view]['LH']['root']=(68,106);D[view]['LH']['joint']=(69,118)
 if view=='se':
  # Finish the unseen sole behind the forearm using the still's own orange
  # shin/ankle paint. Only the existing visible trace can protrude from the torso.
  foot=piece(src['RH'],(Y>=126)&(Y<=134)&(X>=51)&(X<=68))
  comp(src['RH'],shift(foot,21,9))
  foot=piece(src['LH'],Y>=130)
  poly(src['LH'],[(100,129),(105,132),(95,137),(82,140),(78,137),(93,132)],'22301d')
  comp(src['LH'],shift(foot,-25,2))
 if view=='ne':
  # The far fist is occluded by the flank; native far bezel is already visible.
  # Add only a translated native knuckle support block behind the pinned flank.
  kn=piece(src['RF'],Y>=149)
  comp(src['LF'],shift(kn,-59,-20))
 return src,body

  frames=[];rig[view]=[]
  for f in range(8):
   dip=int(f in (0,4));layers={}
   for n,s in src.items():
    sole=support(s);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f];vt=T['views'][view]
    if n in vt['visible_feet']:ideal=np.array(vt['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre'])
    else:ideal=sole+off*np.array(vt['u'])*vt['f']-[0,lift]
    delta=np.floor(ideal-sole+.5).astype(int);dx,dy=map(int,delta)
    a=socket(D[view][n],dx,dy,dip);comp(a,shift(s,dx,dy))
    # The source foot is the only silhouette at the ground, including lifted frames.
    raw=shift(s,dx,dy);bottom=int(sole[1])+dy
    a[Y>bottom]=0;a[(Y>=bottom-1)&(raw[:,:,3]==0)]=0
    layers[n]=a;Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'walk/layers/{view}_{n}_f{f}.png')
    rig[view].append(dict(foot=n,frame=f,delta=[dx,dy],source_sole=sole.tolist(),target=ideal.tolist(),lift=lift,ground_row=bottom+lift,hidden_in_still=n not in vt['visible_feet']))
   a=np.zeros_like(ref)
   for n in ['LF','LH','RH','RF']:comp(a,layers[n])
   comp(a,shift(body,0,dip))
   # Pin transparent background as well as paint above the highest limb socket.
   cut={'e':106,'se':100,'ne':98}[view]+dip
   a[:cut]=shift(ref,0,dip)[:cut]
   # Mechanical socket recesses: enclosed wedges are metal-shadow, never holes.
   holes=fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0);a[holes]=P['22301d']
   frames.append(a);Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'walk/frames/{view}_f{f}.png')
  allframes[view]=frames

"""Pixel audit independent of rig translations and target positions during search."""

def load(p):return np.array(Image.open(p).convert('RGBA'))
def pieces(op):
 seen=np.zeros_like(op);out=[]
 for y,x in zip(*np.where(op)):
  if seen[y,x]:continue
  todo=[(int(y),int(x))];seen[y,x]=True;pts=[]
  while todo:
   yy,xx=todo.pop();pts.append((yy,xx))
   for dy in (-1,0,1):
    for dx in (-1,0,1):
     ny,nx=yy+dy,xx+dx
     if 0<=ny<192 and 0<=nx<192 and op[ny,nx] and not seen[ny,nx]:seen[ny,nx]=True;todo.append((ny,nx))
  out.append(pts)
 return sorted(out,key=len,reverse=True)

def locate(a,src):
 # Search the complete native six-row sole stamp, never the target coordinates.
 sole=b.support(src);ys,xs=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(b.Y>=sole[1]-5));cols=src[ys,xs]
 best=None
 for dy in range(-12,13):
  for dx in range(-14,15):
   score=float(np.all(a[ys+dy,xs+dx]==cols,axis=1).mean())
   key=(score,-abs(dx)-abs(dy))
   if best is None or key>best[0]:best=(key,dx,dy)
 (_,score_unused),dx,dy=best
 score=best[0][0]
 # Both support-band endpoints must still exist, with their actual source colours.
 sy=int(sole[1]);by,bx=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(b.Y>=sy-1));left,right=int(bx.min()),int(bx.max())
 ends=[]
 for x in [left,right]:
  ends.append(any(np.array_equal(a[y+dy,x+dx],src[y,x]) for y in [sy-1,sy] if src[y,x,3]>0))
 return sole+[dx,dy],score,[dx,dy],all(ends)

   err=np.abs(p-target);ground=int(np.floor(target[1]+lift+.5));actual_lift=ground-p[1]
   # Every source paint texel remains at one integer translation in its limb layer.
   sy,sx=np.where(src[:,:,3]>0);dx,dy=delta
   native_match=float(np.all(layer[sy+dy,sx+dx]==src[sy,sx],axis=1).mean())
   # Whole source gem/bezel, palette band and shape. No resampling permitted.
   glow=(src[:,:,1]>180)&(src[:,:,0]<110)&(src[:,:,2]>70)&(src[:,:,3]>0)
   gy,gx=np.where(glow);gemmatch=None
   if len(gy):
    patch=(b.X>=gx.min()-4)&(b.X<=gx.max()+4)&(b.Y>=gy.min()-4)&(b.Y<=gy.max()+4)&(src[:,:,3]>0)
    yy,xx=np.where(patch);gemmatch=float(np.all(layer[yy+dy,xx+dx]==src[yy,xx],axis=1).mean());check(gemmatch==1,f'{v} {n} f{f} whole shield')
   rr=dict(frame=f,measured_sole=p.tolist(),measured_from=where,stamp_match=score,endpoint_visible=ends,recovered_translation=delta,target=target.tolist(),target_error_xy=err.tolist(),expected_lift=lift,measured_lift=actual_lift,native_part_match=native_match,gem_bezel_match=gemmatch)
   pp.append(p);tracks.append(rr);lines.append('CONTACT '+v+' '+n+' '+json.dumps(rr));check(score==1 and ends and err.max()<=1 and lift==actual_lift,f'{v} {n} f{f} contact');check(native_match==1,f'{v} {n} f{f} native part length/paint')
  land=(T['gait'][n]+3)%8;order=[(land+k)%8 for k in range(5)];step=np.array(vt['planted_step_px']);locked=np.array([pp[f]-k*step for k,f in enumerate(order)]);spread=np.ptp(locked,axis=0);check(spread.max()<=2,f'{v} {n} foot lock')
  foot=dict(planted_order=order,spread_xy=spread.tolist(),frames=tracks,max_target_error_xy=np.max([r['target_error_xy'] for r in tracks],axis=0).tolist(),native_part_length_change_px=0)
  vr['feet'][n]=foot;lines.append('FOOTLOCK '+v+' '+n+' '+json.dumps({k:val for k,val in foot.items() if k!='frames'}))
  oy=j*295;dr.text((12,oy+4),f'{v} {n}: stance {order}; lock spread {spread[0]:.3f}/{spread[1]:.3f}px; foot centres from saved pixels',font=font,fill='white')
  colors=[(255,150,80),(80,225,210),(235,130,210),(160,215,80),(180,170,255)];orig=locked.mean(0)
  for k,f in enumerate(order):
   c=colors[k];dr.text((12,oy+30+k*22),f'f{f}: {pp[f][0]:.1f},{pp[f][1]:.1f} -> {locked[k,0]:.3f},{locked[k,1]:.3f}  [{tracks[f]["measured_from"]}]',fill=c,font=font)
   dx,dy=tracks[f]['recovered_translation'];sy=int(source_sole[1]);yy,xx=np.where((src[:,:,3]>0)&(b.Y>=sy-1))
   for y,x in zip(yy,xx):
    # Actual support stamp, moved back into common ground coordinates.
    pt=np.array([x+dx,y+dy])-k*step;px,py=np.floor((pt-orig)*6+[1170,oy+85]).astype(int);dr.rectangle((px,py,px+5,py+5),fill=c)
  # Composites and isolated contacts in adjacent rows, at native 2x.
  cx,cy=source_sole;box=(int(cx)-18,int(cy)-18,int(cx)+18,int(cy)+8)
  for f in range(8):
   xx=12+f*180;dr.text((xx,oy+149),f'f{f}: '+('layer' if 'layer' in tracks[f]['measured_from'] else 'frame'),font=font,fill='white')
   for k,arr in enumerate([frames[f],layers[f]]):
    im=Image.fromarray(arr).crop(box).resize((72,52),Image.Resampling.NEAREST);tile=Image.new('RGBA',im.size,(80,92,83));tile.alpha_composite(im);board.paste(tile.convert('RGB'),(xx+k*78,oy+177))
  dr.text((12,oy+245),'Each cell: final composite / isolated limb. Coloured stamps: five measured support bands corrected by ground step.',font=font,fill=(180,190,180))
 board.save(R/f'{v}_footprints.png')
 rows=[r['lowest_row'] for r in vr['structure']];summary[v]=dict(lowest_rows=rows,upper_diff=[r['upper_body_diff_percent'] for r in vr['structure']],foot_lock={n:q['spread_xy'] for n,q in vr['feet'].items()},max_target_error=np.max([q['max_target_error_xy'] for q in vr['feet'].values()],axis=0).tolist(),occluded_measurements={n:[r['frame'] for r in q['frames'] if 'layer' in r['measured_from']] for n,q in vr['feet'].items()})
 for ext,durations in [('gif',[120,130]*4),('apng',[125]*8)]:
  im=Image.open(R/f'{v}_preview.{ext}');ds=[]
  for f in range(im.n_frames):im.seek(f);ds.append(im.info['duration'])
  check(ds==durations and im.info['loop']==0,f'{v} {ext} timing');lines.append('TIMING '+v+' '+ext+' '+str(ds)+' loop=0')
protected=json.loads((R/'protected_hashes.json').read_text());check(all(hashlib.sha256((R/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==h for p,h in protected.items()),'inputs changed')

Standing fennec at 35 degrees, fennec walk step A (2026-11-05). 160x160, nearest feet on row 140, the 15 palette colours, binary alpha.
Made from Codex's one image (../stand35/sheet_raw.png, 54,066 tokens). Codex finished it so e was 75 px long; that left the head 8% small
(s ear span 43 px against the seated 47, eyes 24/15/8 against 28/18/12). The walk lane re-finished the same raw image with Codex's own
finish.py and only the scale changed (../stand35/refinish/finish_by_ears.py 83): e 83 px long, so the head matches the seated views.
Sizes s 48x64, se 60x68, e 83x66, ne 60x76, n 40x76. Eyes s 29, se 19, e 17, ne 0, n 0. view_check lone 0.10; mirrors e/w 0.915,
ne/nw 0.989, se/sw 0.996; the height REDO flags on ne, n, nw are the same exempt ones the seated run had. Codex's e 75 finish is in codex_e75/.
Known: in n the cream tail tip sits between the hind legs, touching their inner edges (the tail is nearer the camera there, so it is
drawn over them); in ne the far hind leg is mostly hidden. Board: ../stand35/standing_vs_seated.png.

Silhouette-tool target errors: ne 0.43px, s 0.50px, n 2.00px (far fists: wider sidewalls/occlusion).
n fist contact bands use supplied still_sole nine-pixel marks; whole forearms translate vertically, without recentering.
Occluded stamps measured on saved layers: ne LH f0/1/6/7, LF all; s LH f2/3/6/7; n LF f0/1/6/7, RF f2/3/4/5.
Upper-body protected paint difference for f0-7: ne/s/n each [0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0]%. Whole upper canvas also exact.

  n [163,160,160,162,163,160,160,162]. All within allowed row163.

open space beside the far forearm and far arm strip at the silhouette edge, where the ground shows as that arm swings.

"""Rhino cam35 touch-up (sprite touch-up lane, Claude, 2026-11-04).

Preston: "Make a session for touching up sprites using a synthesis of all we've learned all our tools etc.
Especially aseprite. Sel-out, Cap ink, and all the other professional terms or tasks they would use."

Input  (read only): ../views_cam35/{s,se,e,ne,n}.png   (approved by Preston, "Thats it looks good")
Ref    (read only): ../views/{...}.png                  (the original approved stills, the craft standard)
Output:             ../views_cam35_touchup/{...}.png    raw, no outline: the packer adds sel-out at pack time
                    ./board.png, ./metrics.json, ./views_selout_light/ (the light-following sel-out option)

The rule: recolour only. Alpha never changes, so canvas, feet row, offsets and silhouette stay exact.
Passes, in order (each counted separately in metrics.json):
  1. specks   cluster cleanup: yellow clusters of 1-2 px take the colour most of their neighbours have
  2. seams    capped ink on lit faces: a yellow pixel darker than both neighbours across it, with both
              neighbours lit (base or highlight), in a line of 3+ (one-pixel breaks closed), becomes the
              darkest colour #141218: a panel line between plates
  2b. rims    bevel rim: highlight kept on a RIM-deep band along each big highlight plate's lit (top left)
              edges, base yellow inside; ends the "whole plate filled with highlight" look
  3. gaps     charcoal frame: a shadow or deep yellow pixel with a dark line (accent or charcoal, 6+ px) on
              its left or above it sits in that plate's cast shadow (light from the top left) and becomes
              charcoal shadow; capped at CAP x the approved view's frame + seam share
Eyes and the ring around them are never touched.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

import argparse
ROOT = Path(r"D:/dev/new-mech-rpg/docs/art/moba")
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "rhino_l" / "take-a" / "anim"))
from selout_rhino import selout  # noqa: E402
ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("take")                      # e.g. fennec_s/take-b
ap.add_argument("--cap", type=float, default=0.80)
ap.add_argument("--rim", type=int, default=3)
ap.add_argument("--palette", default=None)   # default <take>/cam35/palette.json, else <take>/palette.json
ap.add_argument("--approved", default=None)  # default fixed/ if present, else views/
ap.add_argument("--out", default=None)       # default <take>/views_cam35_touchup (must end in _touchup)
ap.add_argument("--work", default=None)      # default <take>/cam35_touchup (must end in _touchup)
ap.add_argument("--title", default=None)
ap.add_argument("--seam-cap", action="store_true")    # capped ink: no seam ink when the view is already as dark as approved
ap.add_argument("--rim-min", type=int, default=40)  # smallest highlight plate that gets a bevel rim
A = ap.parse_args()
TITLE = A.title or A.take.split("/")[0].split("_")[0].capitalize()
T = ROOT / A.take
OUT = Path(A.out) if A.out else T / "views_cam35_touchup"
WORK = Path(A.work) if A.work else T / "cam35_touchup"
APPROVED = A.approved or ("fixed" if (T / "fixed").is_dir() else "views")
PALF = Path(A.palette) if A.palette else (T / "cam35" / "palette.json" if (T / "cam35" / "palette.json").exists()
                                          else T / "palette.json")
PAL = json.loads(PALF.read_text())

V = ["s", "se", "e", "ne", "n"]
CAP, RIM = A.cap, A.rim
HEX = {p["hex"].lstrip("#").lower(): tuple(int(p["hex"].lstrip("#")[i:i + 2], 16) for i in (0, 2, 4)) for p in PAL}


def _luma(h):
    r, g, b = HEX[h]
    return 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b

def measure_sole(alpha, cx, cy, w, side=3, up=6, down=4):
    H, W = alpha.shape
    xa, xb = max(0, int(math.floor(cx - w / 2 - side))), min(W - 1, int(math.ceil(cx + w / 2 + side)))
    ya, yb = max(0, int(cy) - up), min(H - 1, int(cy) + down)
    lows = {}
    for x in range(xa, xb + 1):
        ys = np.nonzero(alpha[ya:yb + 1, x])[0]
        if len(ys):
            lows[x] = ya + int(ys.max())
    if not lows:
        return None
    seed = min(lows, key=lambda x: abs(lows[x] - cy) + 0.5 * abs(x - cx))
    run = [seed]
    for step in (-1, 1):
        x = seed
        while (x + step) in lows and abs(lows[x + step] - lows[x]) <= 1 and lows[x + step] >= lows[seed] - 2:
            x += step
            run.append(x)
    row = max(lows[x] for x in run)
    cols = [x for x in run if lows[x] >= row - 1]
    return ((min(cols) + max(cols)) / 2, row)


def cmd_footlock(a):
    t = json.load(open(a.targets))
    report, ok_all = {}, True
    still_cols = None
    if a.still_dir:
        still_cols = {}
    for view in (a.views or list(t['views'])):
        vt = t['views'][view]
        frames = [load_rgba(os.path.join(a.frames, a.pattern.format(view=view, i=i))) for i in range(8)]
        alphas = [f[:, :, 3] > 0 for f in frames]
        step = vt['planted_step_px']
        vr = {'feet': {}, 'frames': []}
        print(f'== {view}: planted step {step}')
        for foot, fd in vt['visible_feet'].items():
            w = fd['still_sole'][1] - fd['still_sole'][0] + 1
            meas, err = [], 0.0
            for fr in fd['frames']:
                m = measure_sole(alphas[fr['frame']], fr['sole_centre'][0], fr['sole_centre'][1], w)
                meas.append(m)
                if m:
                    err = max(err, abs(m[0] - fr['sole_centre'][0]), abs(m[1] - fr['sole_centre'][1]))
            planted = [fr['lift'] == 0 for fr in fd['frames']]
            start = next(i for i in range(8) if planted[i] and not planted[(i - 1) % 8])
            run = []
            i = start
            while planted[i] and len(run) < 8:
                run.append(i)
                i = (i + 1) % 8
            corr = [(meas[i][0] - j * step[0], meas[i][1] - j * step[1]) for j, i in enumerate(run) if meas[i]]
            sx = max(c[0] for c in corr) - min(c[0] for c in corr) if corr else None
            sy = max(c[1] for c in corr) - min(c[1] for c in corr) if corr else None
            ok = corr and len(corr) == len(run) and sx <= 2 and sy <= 2
            ok_all &= bool(ok)
            vr['feet'][foot] = {'planted_frames': run, 'spread_px': [sx, sy], 'max_target_error_px': round(err, 2),
                                'measured': meas, 'pass': bool(ok)}
            print(f'   {foot}: planted f{run}, lock spread x {sx:.2f} y {sy:.2f} px, '
                  f'max off target {err:.2f} px -> {"pass" if ok else "CHECK"}')
        still = None
        if a.still_dir:
            still = load_rgba(os.path.join(a.still_dir, f'{view}.png'), a.still_offset, frames[0].shape[0])
            sc = still[still[:, :, 3] > 0][:, :3]
            still_set = {tuple(c) for c in sc}
        for i, (fr, al) in enumerate(zip(frames, alphas)):
            alpha_raw = fr[:, :, 3]
            lowest = int(np.nonzero(al.any(axis=1))[0].max())
            semi = int(((alpha_raw > 0) & (alpha_raw < 255)).sum())
            edge = bool(al[0].any() or al[-1].any() or al[:, 0].any() or al[:, -1].any())
            pieces = count_pieces(al)
            row = {'frame': i, 'lowest_row': lowest, 'semi_alpha_px': semi, 'canvas_edge': edge,
                   'pieces': len(pieces), 'stray_px': sum(pieces[1:])}
            if still is not None:
                fc = fr[al][:, :3]
                row['off_palette_px'] = int(sum(1 for c in map(tuple, fc) if c not in still_set))
            vr['frames'].append(row)
        print('   frames: ' + '; '.join(
            f"f{r['frame']} low {r['lowest_row']} pieces {r['pieces']}"
            + (f" stray {r['stray_px']}" if r['stray_px'] else '')
            + (f" semi {r['semi_alpha_px']}" if r['semi_alpha_px'] else '')
            + (' EDGE' if r['canvas_edge'] else '')
            + (f" offpal {r['off_palette_px']}" if r.get('off_palette_px') else '') for r in vr['frames']))
        report[view] = vr
    if a.out:
        json.dump(report, open(a.out, 'w'), indent=1)
    print('ALL FEET LOCKED' if ok_all else 'SOME FEET NEED A LOOK')


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- seams
BODY_GREENS = {(0x22, 0x30, 0x1d), (0x47, 0x69, 0x3a), (0x6c, 0x9b, 0x4a), (0x9c, 0xcb, 0x62)}  # gorilla body bands


def find_slits(fr, st, dip, n=6):
    """Pixels solid in the idle still (after the dip) but now transparent and enclosed within n px on all four sides:
    the gap a limb leaves when it slides back from a pinned body (gorilla run 1 and 2, 2026-11-05)."""
    a = fr[:, :, 3] > 0
    s = np.zeros_like(a)
    s[dip:] = st[:st.shape[0] - dip, :, 3] > 0
    out = []
    for y, x in zip(*np.nonzero(~a & s)):
        if a[y, max(0, x - n):x].any() and a[y, x + 1:x + 1 + n].any() and a[max(0, y - n):y, x].any() \
                and a[y + 1:y + 1 + n, x].any():
            out.append((int(x), int(y)))
    return out


def fill_colour(fr, st, dip, x, y, done, body):
    """The band on both sides if they agree, else the still's own body colour there, else the next body colour right."""
    row = fr[y]
    op = row[:, 3] > 0
    left = next((tuple(row[i, :3]) for i in range(x - 1, -1, -1) if op[i] or (i, y) in done), None)
    right = next((tuple(row[i, :3]) for i in range(x + 1, row.shape[0]) if op[i]), None)
    if left == right and left in body:
        return left
    sc = tuple(st[y - dip, x, :3]) if st[y - dip, x, 3] else None
    if sc in body:
        return sc
    for i in range(x + 1, row.shape[0]):
        if op[i] and tuple(row[i, :3]) in body:
            return tuple(row[i, :3])
    return right or left


def cmd_seams(a):
    t = json.load(open(a.targets))
    body = {tuple(bytes.fromhex(c)) for c in a.body} if a.body else BODY_GREENS
    if a.fix:
        os.makedirs(a.fix, exist_ok=True)
    for view in (a.views or list(t['views'])):
        st = load_rgba(os.path.join(a.still_dir, f'{view}.png'))
        counts = []
        for i in range(8):
            name = a.pattern.format(view=view, i=i)
            fr = load_rgba(os.path.join(a.frames, name))
            dip = 1 if i in t['dip_frames'] else 0
            sl = find_slits(fr, st, dip)
            counts.append(len(sl))
            if a.fix:
                done = set()
                for x, y in sorted(sl, key=lambda q: (q[1], q[0])):
                    fr[y, x, :3] = fill_colour(fr, st, dip, x, y, done, body)
                    fr[y, x, 3] = 255
                    done.add((x, y))
                Image.fromarray(fr).save(os.path.join(a.fix, name))
        print(f'{view}: enclosed slit px per frame {counts}' + (' (filled)' if a.fix else ''))
    print('Look at every slit before filling: a real gap between a limb and the body must stay open.')


"""Sel-out for the MOBA style, baked into the rhino's frames (Claude, 2026-11-04). Preston: "ok now sel out for
our moba style using what we've collected".

The MOBA creatures follow the Hollowtide sprite canon, where the outline comes from the sel-out shader
(hollowtide docs/art/crew-dungeon-selout-2026-10-10/candidate/scripts/arena/sel_out.gdshaderinc) instead of being
painted. This game has no such shader, so its creature rules are applied here per pixel, exactly:
  1. a transparent pixel whose up, down, left or right neighbour is a light or material colour (luma 0.23 or more)
     becomes the outline colour: one texel of outline outside the silhouette;
  2. a silhouette edge pixel (a transparent pixel up, down, left or right of it) darker than luma 0.23 is darkened
     to the outline colour (per channel minimum), so existing dark edges get no extra thickness;
  3. the shader's speck cleanup only merges colours closer than 0.12 with luma over 0.16; no two rhino colours
     are that close, so it changes nothing here and is left out; its detail-ink mode is off for creatures.
Outline colour: the rhino palette's darkest, #141218 (the shader's #0B0E11 would add a 13th colour; they differ by
under 10 per channel). The feet still stand on y=160; under a light foot edge the outline sits on y=161.
Reads every sheet's frames/ and writes frames_selout/ beside it, so rerun after any fix to the frames.
"""
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ANIM = Path(__file__).parent
OUTLINE = np.array([0x14, 0x12, 0x18], np.uint8)
SHEETS = [ANIM / "codex-set-a" / s for s in ("idle", "walk", "horn-jab", "horn-toss")] + \
         [ANIM / "codex-set-b" / s for s in ("brace", "stampede", "death")] + [ANIM / "codex-ground-pound/clean"] + \
         [ANIM / "codex-set-c" / s for s in ("stampede", "horn-toss", "ground-pound", "horn-jab")] + \
         [ANIM / "codex-set-d" / s for s in ("brace", "death", "idle", "brace-shuffle")]  # se/ne rows, shuffle


def selout(a):
    op = a[..., 3] > 127
    luma = (a[..., :3].astype(float) / 255) @ np.array([0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722])
    light = op & (luma >= 0.23)

    def nb(m, fill):
        p = np.pad(m, 1, constant_values=fill)
        return p[:-2, 1:-1], p[2:, 1:-1], p[1:-1, :-2], p[1:-1, 2:]

    repair = ~op & np.logical_or.reduce(nb(light, False))
    edge = op & np.logical_or.reduce([~x for x in nb(op, False)])
    dark_edge = edge & (luma < 0.23)
    out = a.copy()
    out[dark_edge, :3] = np.minimum(a[dark_edge, :3], OUTLINE)
    out[repair, :3] = OUTLINE
    out[repair, 3] = 255
    return out, int(repair.sum()), int(dark_edge.sum())


def main():
    total, pairs = [], []
    for d in SHEETS:
        src, dst = d / "frames", d / "frames_selout"
        dst.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        for p in sorted(src.glob("*_f[0-9].png")):
            a = np.asarray(Image.open(p).convert("RGBA"))
            b, added, darkened = selout(a)
            Image.fromarray(b).save(dst / p.name)
            total.append((d.name, p.stem, added, darkened))
            if p.stem in ("front_f0", "side_f0", "back_f0") and d.name == "idle" or \
               (d.name, p.stem) in {("horn-jab", "side_f3"), ("stampede", "front_f3"), ("death", "side_f4")}:
                pairs.append((f"{d.name} {p.stem}", a, b))
    added = [t[2] for t in total]
    print(f"{len(total)} frames; outline pixels added per frame {min(added)}-{max(added)}; "
          f"dark edge pixels darkened {sum(t[3] for t in total)} in all")
    # before | after at 4x on the MOBA map grey
    k, box = 4, (24, 72, 168, 166)
    w, h = (box[2] - box[0]) * k, (box[3] - box[1]) * k
    board = Image.new("RGBA", (2 * w + 8, len(pairs) * (h + 16)), (0x5A, 0x60, 0x58, 255))
    dr = ImageDraw.Draw(board)
    for j, (lab, a, b) in enumerate(pairs):
        y = j * (h + 16)
        dr.text((4, y + 2), f"{lab}   left: before   right: sel-out", fill=(240, 240, 240, 255))
        for i, im in enumerate((a, b)):
            board.alpha_composite(Image.fromarray(im).crop(box).resize((w, h), Image.NEAREST), (i * (w + 8), y + 16))
    board.save(ANIM / "selout-board.png")
    print("board", board.size)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def rects(boxes):
 m=np.zeros((192,192),bool)
 for x0,y0,x1,y1 in boxes:m[y0:y1+1,x0:x1+1]=True
 return m

def dark(a):
 a=a.copy()
 for c,d in [('e0c8ff','a46bff'),('a46bff','6a3cc8'),('6a3cc8','2e1a6e'),('d6d6dc','9d9ba2'),('9d9ba2','3d3a44')]:
  m=np.all(a==P[c],axis=2);# use original to avoid cascading
  m=np.all(orig_dark==P[c],axis=2) if False else m
 # simultaneous palette mapping
 old=a.copy()
 for c,d in [('e0c8ff','a46bff'),('a46bff','6a3cc8'),('6a3cc8','2e1a6e'),('d6d6dc','9d9ba2'),('9d9ba2','3d3a44')]:a[np.all(old==P[c],axis=2)]=P[d]
 return a

def sources(v):
 ref=load(R/f'stills/{v}.png');used=np.zeros((192,192),bool);parts={}
 for n in ['RF','RH','LF','LH']:
  if n not in D[v]:continue
  q=D[v][n];km=b.mask(q['knee'])&~used&(ref[:,:,3]>0);lm=b.mask(q['lower'])&~used&~km&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
  parts[n]={'knee':b.piece(ref,km),'lower':b.piece(ref,lm)};used|=km|lm
 # Hidden legs reconstructed with the same translated native parts, one ramp darker.
 # Their still peeks set depth/foot position. No pasted fragment can become detached.
 hidden={'e':[('LH','RH',6,-7),('LF','RF',8,-6)],'se':[('LH','RH',10,-5)],'ne':[('LF','RF',-12,-5)]}[v]
 for n,fromn,dx,dy in hidden:
  q=dict(D[v][fromn]);q['root']=tuple(np.array(q['root'])+[dx,dy]);q['k']=tuple(np.array(q['k'])+[dx,dy]);q['join']=tuple(np.array(q['join'])+[dx,dy]);q['sole']=tuple(np.array(q['sole'])+[dx,dy]);q['hidden']=True;D[v][n]=q
  parts[n]={p:dark(b.shift(a,dx,dy)) for p,a in parts[fromn].items()}
 # Use the actual visible far sole where there is enough source to recover it.
 if v=='se':
  p=b.piece(ref,rects([(91,142,96,148)]));parts['LH']['lower']=p;D[v]['LH']['sole']=(94,148);D[v]['LH']['join']=(94,142)
 tail=b.piece(ref,b.mask(TAIL[v])&~used)
 used|=tail[:,:,3]>0;used|=rects(PEEKS[v])&(ref[:,:,3]>0)
 body=b.piece(ref,~used)
 # Finish the flank behind the native hip housings. Only body above the leg
 # attachment is filled; the space between shin/foot pairs remains background.
 # Nearest native body band copied by row, as in the gorilla's seam repair.
 fills={'e':[(82,132,96,136),(108,131,114,134)],'se':[(81,127,89,130),(98,133,105,136)],'ne':[(90,135,99,138),(107,124,113,127)]}[v]
 for x0,y0,x1,y1 in fills:
  for y in range(y0,y1+1):
   for x in range(x0,x1+1):
    if body[y,x,3]==0:body[y,x]=P['2e1a6e']
 return ref,parts,body,tail

def band(a,p0,p1,width=6,far=False):
 # Native palette bands, stepped by <=1px per row wherever room permits.
 x0,y0=p0;x1,y1=p1;y0,y1=int(y0),int(y1)
 if y1<y0:x0,y0,x1,y1=x1,y1,x0,y0
 for y in range(y0,y1+1):
  t=(y-y0)/max(1,y1-y0);cx=int(np.floor(x0+(x1-x0)*t+.5));l=cx-width//2;r=l+width
  a[y,l:r]=P['2e1a6e'];a[y,l+1:r-1]=P['6a3cc8' if not far else '2e1a6e']
  a[y,l+1:l+3]=P['a46bff' if not far else '6a3cc8'];a[y,r-1]=P['141218']

def make(v):
 for d in ['walk/frames','walk/layers','source']:(R/d).mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 ref,parts,body,tail=sources(v)
 for label,a in [('body',body),('tail',tail)]:Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'source/{v}_{label}.png')
 for n,pr in parts.items():
  for p,a in pr.items():Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'source/{v}_{n}_{p}.png')
 frames=[];rig=[]
 for f in range(8):
  dip=int(f in (0,4));layers={}
  for n,pr in parts.items():
   q=D[v][n];sole=np.array(q['sole'],float);off,lift=T['paths'][n][f];vt=T['views'][v]
   ideal=np.array(vt['visible_feet'][n]['frames'][f]['sole_centre']) if n in vt['visible_feet'] else sole+off*np.array(vt['u'])*vt['f']-[0,lift]
   dx,dy=np.floor(ideal-sole+.5).astype(int);kx=int(np.floor(dx*.5+.5));ky=int(np.floor((dy+dip)*.5+.5))
   a=np.zeros_like(ref);root=np.array(q['root'])+[0,dip];knee=np.array(q['k'])+[kx,ky];join=np.array(q['join'])+[dx,dy]
   band(a,root,knee,width=7 if n.endswith('H') else 6,far=n.startswith('L'))
   # The shin and foot remain one rigid native translation. Draw the small
   # articulated connection UNDER both translated pieces so no butt seam shows.
   band(a,knee,join,width=6,far=n.startswith('L'))
   b.comp(a,b.shift(pr['lower'],dx,dy));b.comp(a,b.shift(pr['knee'],kx,ky))
   bottom=int(sole[1]+dy);a[Y>bottom]=0
   raw=b.shift(pr['lower'],dx,dy);a[(Y>=bottom-1)&(raw[:,:,3]==0)]=0
   layers[n]=a;Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'walk/layers/{v}_{n}_f{f}.png')
   rig.append(dict(foot=n,frame=f,delta=[int(dx),int(dy)],knee_delta=[kx,ky],source_sole=sole.tolist(),target=ideal.tolist(),lift=lift,hidden_in_still=n not in vt['visible_feet']))
  a=np.zeros_like(ref)
  # Tail always behind legs. Pinned trunk hides far legs only where it really exists.
  b.comp(a,b.shift(tail,0,int(f in (1,5))))
  for n in ['LF','LH']:b.comp(a,layers[n])
  b.comp(a,b.shift(body,0,dip))
  for n in ['RF','RH']:b.comp(a,layers[n])
  # Above the attachments, pin both paint and transparent background exactly.
  cut={'e':131,'se':127,'ne':124}[v]+dip
  pinned=b.shift(ref,0,dip);tt=b.shift(tail,0,dip);pinned[tt[:,:,3]>0]=0;b.comp(pinned,b.shift(tail,0,int(f in (1,5))))
  a[:cut]=pinned[:cut]
  # Fill only actual enclosed holes: translated native body paint where available,
  # deepest violet in mechanical socket recesses. No blanket silhouette expansion.
  holes=b.fill_holes(a[:,:,3]>0)&(a[:,:,3]==0)
  st=b.shift(ref,0,dip)
  for yy,xx in zip(*np.where(holes)):
   c=tuple(st[yy,xx]);a[yy,xx]=c if c in [P[h] for h in ['e0c8ff','a46bff','6a3cc8','2e1a6e']] else P['2e1a6e']
  Image.fromarray(a).save(R/f'walk/frames/{v}_f{f}.png');frames.append(a)
 old=json.loads((R/'rig.json').read_text()) if (R/'rig.json').exists() else {};old[v]=rig;(R/'rig.json').write_text(json.dumps(old,indent=2))
 boards(v,ref,frames)
 report=R/'REPORT.txt';s=report.read_text() if report.exists() else 'fennec-trot35-run1. Tokens: final total unavailable.\\nRF/RH near right; LF/LH far left; supplied labels retained.\\n'
 s+=f'{v}: eight native frames drawn; measurements and visual review pending.\\n';report.write_text(s)
 print(v,'drawn')

def boards(v,ref,frames):
 sel=b.load_selout();font=ImageFont.truetype('C:/Windows/Fonts/arial.ttf',18);bg=(74,80,76,255)
